# Third-party artwork notices

Brand names, marks, and logos belong to their respective owners. The package's MIT/Apache licenses apply to its implementation; they do not grant rights to third-party trademarks or replace the owners' brand-use terms.

## MCP Server symbol

The MCP Server symbol reproduces the official Model Context Protocol mark from [the project's original SVG](https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/docs/blob/573dc60c2e7aab2605b29d0bf27194aa7b02e4fb/favicon.svg), distributed under the MIT License. Copyright (c) 2024–2025 Anthropic, PBC and contributors. The original SVG is retained in `src/hash-icon/mcp-server.svg`; its three paths, round caps, and stroke proportions are unchanged. The component scales the artwork uniformly from its 180-unit canvas to 24 units and uses `currentColor` instead of black. The license is included in `licenses/Model-Context-Protocol-MIT.txt`.

## Integration artwork

The existing HASH integration artwork comes from `hashintel/internal-sites` (AGPL-3.0). Additional vector artwork comes from the brands' sites and the linked archives. The `gilbarbara/logos` collection uses CC0; `pheralb/svgl`, `devicons/devicon`, and `edent/SuperTinyIcons` use MIT. Those collection licenses do not grant trademark rights. The package preserves the artwork's provenance below.

Gmail full-logo files embed authentic PNG artwork. Microsoft Teams combines its product mark with outlined Segoe UI Semibold lettering, matching the other Microsoft product logos. Veeva Vault pairs its mark with the Veeva corporate wordmark. BigQuery and Redshift use archived product lock-ups. Apple, Apple Notes, and Apple Health have no verified separate full-logo assets and expose only mark and tile variants.

Celonis, Veeva Vault, Oracle, Messenger, Instagram, Zoom, and GitHub lock-ups combine the sourced mark and wordmark; they are compositions, not downloaded complete lock-ups. The Celonis and Oracle marks omit their embedded lettering to avoid repeating the name. Their original mark assets remain available in the mark and tile variants.

Teams, Word, Outlook, Excel, PowerPoint, OneNote, and OneDrive use Microsoft's October 2025 product marks. Copilot uses the two-part 2026 mark, verified against [Microsoft's design announcement](https://microsoft.design/articles/more-than-a-mark/) and live product site. These marks are distributed via Wikimedia Commons (marked PD-textlogo there). Their full-logo compositions use the product names outlined in [Microsoft's Segoe UI Semibold 5.32](https://c.s-microsoft.com/static/fonts/segoe-ui/west-european/Semibold/latest.woff2). They follow the Teams layout and light/dark text treatment; they are not downloaded official lock-ups. Only the lettering outlines ship, not the font.

## Additional company and product identities

The following artwork was retrieved on 2026-11-05. Google Workspace full logos are compositions using outlined Google Sans Regular; no font files ship. Amazon S3 has no verified full-logo variant. Clay’s official kit embeds a raster mark: this package vectorizes that mark while retaining the native wordmark. X and legacy Twitter have no verified separate full-logo lock-ups.

## Additional AWS product identities

These entries use standalone product artwork. DynamoDB, RDS, and CloudFront retain archived dimensional product marks, matching the existing Redshift and S3 treatment. EC2 uses its stacked-square mark; Lambda uses the solid orange lambda; Amplify uses its documentation mark and purple palette; Amazon Q uses its gradient hexagonal identity. S3, EC2, DynamoDB, RDS, CloudFront, Lambda, and Amazon Q have no verified full-logo variant. Amplify reproduces its officially published lock-up. AWS and Amazon Pay use native official vector logos.

## Simple Icons

Artwork from Simple Icons is used under CC0 1.0 Universal, at commit `e451c581fccb01b56f47e1a77292bdd1fd95b1a4`. Collection licensing does not grant trademark rights. The applicable CC0 dedication is retained in `licenses/simple-icons-CC0.txt`.

## QuantumBlack

The QuantumBlack mark comes from McKinsey’s QuantumBlack design system at commit `c8cf9501844bd5d8553227d5b6faab153a1abf6d`. The original mark was cropped to its visible artwork; dark variants adapt the neutral color. The upstream notice is retained in `licenses/quantumblack-NOTICE.txt`. The Apache 2.0 terms are reproduced in `LICENSE-APACHE.md`.

## Dashboard Icons

Apple Maps’ dark appearance uses vector geometry from `homarr-labs/dashboard-icons`, retrieved on 2026-11-05, under Apache 2.0. The source is `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/homarr-labs/dashboard-icons/main/svg/apple-maps.svg`. XML styles were normalized and the view box trimmed. The license is retained in `licenses/dashboard-icons-Apache-2.0.txt`; trademark rights remain with the owner.

## Docker and Johnson & Johnson

Docker and its logo are trademarks of Docker, Inc. in the United States and/or other countries. Docker artwork comes from its official media kit.

Johnson & Johnson artwork credit: Johnson & Johnson.

## Font Awesome catalogue additions

Font Awesome Free 7.3.1 artwork is copyright 2026 Fonticons, Inc. and distributed under [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/). The upstream notice is included in `licenses/FontAwesome-Free.txt`. Downloadable SVGs derived from this artwork retain their attribution. Adaptations normalize bounds, apply theme colors, and compose app tiles. No endorsement is implied.

The catalogue groups alternate, square, circle, and card glyphs under one brand, preserves existing identities, and omits generic symbols, license-condition pictograms, and fictional identities. `src/integration-icon/fontawesome-coverage.json` records the comparison. Monochrome source artwork remains monochrome where a brand palette was not verified. Full-logo slots are provided only where a wordmark or complete primary logo is available.

## Additional consumer, publishing, finance, transport, and entertainment brands

This expansion adds 281 identities, with 260 verified full-logo assets. Source metadata is retained here and in the source catalogue. Theme palettes, bounded marks, and translucent app tiles are adaptations. Native vector artwork is preferred; a small number of raster-only wordmarks and emblems were vectorized from the cited source.

Additional collection licenses are included in `licenses/theSVG-MIT.txt`, `licenses/Soaring-Symbols-MIT.txt`, and `licenses/Cardog-Icons-MIT.txt`. Collection licenses do not grant trademark rights.

## Additional airline brands

This expansion adds 71 airline identities, each with seven mark, app-tile, and full-logo variants. Artwork is adapted from Soaring Symbols, copyright (c) 2024 Anh Thang, under the MIT license in `licenses/Soaring-Symbols-MIT.txt`. Source files and the pinned collection revision are recorded for each brand in `src/integration-icon/brands.json`. Bounds, dark-theme palettes, mono contrast, and app-tile composition are adaptations. The Qantas tail and China Airlines blossom are isolated from their complete vector lock-ups. All new SVG exports retain the collection copyright and license text. No trademark rights are granted.

## Additional luxury brands

This expansion adds 62 fashion, watch, jewellery, and luxury-goods identities, each with seven mark, app-tile, and full-logo variants. Per-brand source URLs and adaptation notes remain in `src/integration-icon/brands.json`. Source artwork comes from the brands’ public websites, Wikimedia-hosted vector reproductions, and Worldvectorlogo. White backgrounds are removed, emblems are isolated where available, and theme palettes and app tiles are adapted. McQueen’s current wordmark and Breitling’s B emblem are vectorized from official raster artwork; no substitute typefaces or embedded bitmaps are used. Kering’s press-library artwork is provided for editorial use only. No trademark rights are granted, and owners’ brand-use terms continue to apply.

## Additional UK brands

The catalogue includes 102 additional current UK brands across finance, communications, media, retail, consumer products, transport, fashion, and industry, plus separately labeled legacy ITV, Pearson, and Vauxhall identities. Artwork comes from public brand-owned assets, Wikimedia vector reproductions, Simple Icons, and public vector-logo archives; exact sources and adaptation notes remain in the source metadata. The Sky spectrum color field is reconstructed with native SVG gradients inside the source vector outline. No third-party trademark rights are granted. BrewDog, Liberty London, legacy Pearson, and legacy Vauxhall have mark/app variants only where no corresponding verified full logo is included.

[berry-bros-window]: https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/60d1cb13763e9a2b36d15fe0/def9d4c1-a69a-44db-a1b7-8a88013a80d4/berry-bros-%26-rudd-spirits-specialist-whisky-rum-gin-logo.png

## Additional US brands

This expansion adds 86 current US-origin company and product identities and the separately labeled historical 21st Century Fox corporate identity. The former 20th Century Fox studio identity remains legacy; current 20th Century Studios is a distinct entry. Related search names do not imply that these companies or identities are interchangeable. Source URLs and adaptation notes remain in the source metadata. Bounds, theme palettes, mono contrast and app tiles are adapted from outlined brand artwork. Target and Chevrolet have mark/app variants only where no separate verified full wordmark lock-up is included.

The Popeyes wordmark vector is by Tucrack, licensed under [Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/). The adapted Popeyes artwork is distributed under the same license; its bounds and theme colors are adjusted and app tiles composed. Oreo artwork supplied by Brandslogos.com is provided for personal, non-commercial or editorial use. Third-party artwork remains subject to its original license and brand-use terms; the package's software licenses do not replace them. No trademark rights are granted.

## European brand expansion

The European expansion adds 128 current identities across retail, transport, finance, technology, food and drink, industry, energy, and other sectors. Artwork is retained as SVG outlines from official brand sites, Wikimedia archives, Simple Icons, the CC0 logos collection, and vector archives. Individual source URLs and adaptation notes remain in `src/integration-icon/brands.json`. Symbols are isolated from complete lock-ups where available; typography is not recreated with substitute fonts. Seven entries expose only mark and app variants because a separate full logo has not been included. Safran’s source contains a small raster highlight, preserved as vector rectangles. Automated check dates record artwork processing, not trademark-owner approval. Geographic profiles are provisional and do not treat a brand’s country of origin as its complete market footprint.

## Consultancy, semiconductor, satellite, and HashiCorp brands

This expansion adds 77 current identities and three legacy identities. The requested consultancy and consumer brands accompany 30 semiconductor, equipment, and design-tool companies, 27 current satellite operators and manufacturers, and 12 current HashiCorp product identities. Maxar, HCP Vault Secrets, and HCP Consul Dedicated are separately labeled legacy. Artwork comes from official brand websites and HashiCorp’s brand downloads, Wikimedia vector reproductions, Simple Icons, the CC0 logos collection, and public vector archives. Exact URLs and adaptation notes remain in the source metadata. Telesat and Lanteris lettering is faithfully vectorized from source rasters; no replacement typefaces are used. Sentinel uses HashiCorp’s corporate mark as its official pages do, without an invented product wordmark. Bounds, theme colors, mono contrast and app tiles are adapted. No third-party trademark rights are granted.

Amplify Partners uses its official website’s outlined wordmark and striped-A mark, without the reference image’s colored background. “Amplify Ventures” is an integration-name alias for this identity.

## Restaurant and web platform identities

This addition includes 32 current identities and the separately labeled earlier Automattic wordmark. Artwork is retained as native SVG paths where available; Applebee’s, Zaxby’s and the current Automattic symbol are vector conversions of sourced raster artwork. Nobu’s circular emblem is extracted from an official vector menu. Exact source URLs and adaptation notes remain in the source metadata. No third-party trademark rights are granted.

Applebee’s artwork is attributed to Applebee’s and licensed under Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 2.5 (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/2.5/). Vector conversion, isolated mark, bounds, theme colors, and app tiles are adaptations distributed under the same license. Applebee’s trademarks remain the property of their owner.
